HEARTHLANDS: AGE OF TILLAGE — FULL RULES TUTORIAL
3000 pages total · rendered with players: Mike, Dana, Rosa
Each page is one phone screen. Tier = absurdity level (0 = sounds real, 4 = fully absurd).


======================================================================
CHAPTER 1 OF 42: Setting Up the Game
======================================================================

--- Page 1 · WELCOME · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WELCOME TO THE HEARTHLANDS
Welcome, Mike, Dana and Rosa. In Hearthlands: Age of Tillage, each player leads a farming Household competing to become the most Respected in the valley.
This short tutorial will teach you everything you need for your first game.
[icons: rulebook, cottage, barley]

--- Page 2 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE OBJECT OF THE GAME
Earn Respect by building Cottages, Granaries, and Mills, by connecting your Household with Cart Roads, and by trading wisely.
The first Household to reach 10 Respect wins.
[icons: respect, cottage, granary]

--- Page 3 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WHAT'S IN THE BOX
19 terrain hexes, 95 Resource cards, 5 sets of Household pieces, 1 Reeve, 1 Harvest Die, and this rulebook.
[icons: barley_rise, card_back, harvest_die, reeve]

--- Page 4 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE FIVE RESOURCES
Barley, Flax, Mutton, Peat, and Wicker.
Each comes from a different type of terrain, and each is used to build different things.
[icons: barley, flax, mutton, peat, wicker]

--- Page 5 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE TERRAIN
Barley Rise produces Barley. Flax Fields produce Flax. Mutton Downs produce Mutton. The Peat Bog produces Peat. Wicker Marsh produces Wicker.
[icons: barley_rise, mutton_downs, peat_bog, wicker_marsh]

--- Page 6 · COMPONENT · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
YOUR HOUSEHOLD PIECES
Each player takes the pieces in their color: 5 Cottages, 4 Granaries, 2 Mills, and 15 Cart Roads.
[icons: cottage, granary, mill, cart_road]

--- Page 7 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
CHOOSING COLORS
Players choose from red, blue, cream, orange, and green.
Mike chooses first, then play proceeds clockwise.
[icons: pawn_red, pawn_blue, pawn_cream, pawn_orange, pawn_green]

--- Page 8 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BUILDING THE BOARD
Shuffle the terrain hexes and arrange them face-up in a large hexagon.
Place a number token on each hex. The Peat Bog in the center receives no number.
[icons: peat_bog, barley_rise, wicker_marsh]

--- Page 9 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PLACING THE REEVE
The Reeve begins the game on the center hex.
While the Reeve is on a hex, that hex does not produce resources.
[icons: reeve]

--- Page 10 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
STARTING POSITIONS
In turn order, each player places one Cottage and one attached Cart Road on the board.
Then, in reverse order, each player places a second Cottage and Cart Road.
[icons: cottage, cart_road]

--- Page 11 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
STARTING RESOURCES
Each player takes one Resource card for each hex touching their second Cottage.
[icons: card_back, cottage]

--- Page 12 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
TAKING A TURN
Each turn has three phases: the Production Phase, the Trade Phase, and the Build Phase.
Play proceeds clockwise, beginning with Mike.
[icons: harvest_die, card_back, cottage]

--- Page 13 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE PRODUCTION PHASE
Roll the Harvest Die. Every hex showing that number produces its resource.
Each player with a Cottage touching that hex takes one card. Each player with a Granary takes two.
[icons: harvest_die, barley]

--- Page 14 · EXAMPLE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Mike rolls a 6. The Barley Rise showing 6 produces. Mike has a Cottage there and takes 1 Barley. Dana has a Granary there and takes 2 Barley.
[icons: harvest_die, barley, cottage, granary]

--- Page 15 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
ROLLING A 7
When a 7 is rolled, no hexes produce. Instead, any player with more than seven Resource cards discards half of them.
The active player then moves the Reeve to a new hex.
[icons: harvest_die, reeve]

--- Page 16 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE TRADE PHASE
The active player may trade Resource cards with any other player, on any terms both agree to.
They may also trade with the supply at a rate of 4 identical cards for 1 card of their choice.
[icons: card_back, flax, mutton]

--- Page 17 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE BUILD PHASE
The active player may spend resources to build as many pieces as they can afford.
[icons: cottage, cart_road]

--- Page 18 · TABLE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
BUILDING COSTS
Cart Road: 1 Wicker, 1 Peat
Cottage: 1 Wicker, 1 Peat, 1 Barley, 1 Mutton
Granary (upgrades a Cottage): 2 Barley, 3 Flax
Mill: 1 Barley, 1 Flax, 2 Peat
[icons: cart_road, cottage, granary, mill]

--- Page 19 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
PLACEMENT RULES
Cottages must be placed on an intersection connected to one of your Cart Roads.
No Cottage may be placed adjacent to another Cottage.
[icons: cottage, cart_road]

--- Page 20 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
SCORING RESPECT
Each Cottage is worth 1 Respect. Each Granary is worth 2 Respect. Each Mill is worth 1 Respect.
[icons: respect, cottage, granary, mill]

--- Page 21 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE LONGEST CART ROAD
The first player to build a continuous Cart Road of five or more segments earns 2 bonus Respect.
If another player builds a longer one, the bonus moves to them.
[icons: cart_road, respect]

--- Page 22 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WINNING THE GAME
The game ends immediately when a player reaches 10 Respect on their turn. That player wins.
[icons: respect]

--- Page 23 · NOTE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
YOU'RE READY TO LEARN THE DETAILS
That covers the core game, Mike! The following chapters cover the remaining rules, variants, and edge cases.
Most groups find them helpful.
[icons: rulebook]

--- Page 24 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
WEATHER
At the start of each round, roll the Weather Cube. Its result affects production for that round.
Sun: no effect. Rain: Barley Rise produces double. Fog: no trading with the supply.
[icons: weather_cube]

--- Page 25 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
THE TAX ASSESSOR
The Tax Assessor is a second neutral piece. It moves one hex clockwise each round.
Players with a building next to the Tax Assessor pay one resource of their choice.
[icons: assessor]

--- Page 26 · RULE · Tier 0 (Straight-faced) ---
ALMANAC CARDS
Almanac cards may be bought for 1 Flax, 1 Mutton, and 1 Barley.
Each card has a one-time effect. You may play one Almanac card per turn.
[icons: almanac]

--- Page 27 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
We playtested this rule for eleven years. We think it's close.

--- Page 28 · EXAMPLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Dana has 4 Mutton and a Weather Cube. During the Levy, the Tax Assessor moves onto their Loamfields. Because the Weather Cube shows Fog, Rosa must flip the Weather Cube to Drizzle.
Mike records it on a Ledger page. Play continues.
[icons: mutton, weather_cube, loamfields]

--- Page 29 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.29
When a trade involves exactly one Wicker, the player with the fewest Wells must flip the Weather Cube to Drizzle, unless they have no Resource cards.
[icons: well, wicker]

--- Page 30 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.30
Whenever the Tax Assessor ends its movement on the Flax Fields, the player to the left of the active player must record the transaction on a Ledger page, unless it is the first round of High Harvest.
[icons: flax_fields]

--- Page 31 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: What if two players both want to be the Reeve?
A: The Reeve is not a player. Please stop asking.

--- Page 32 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE K-1
Control of the Brine Flats: 4 Respect
Control of the Brine Flats: 6 Respect
Each Well: −5 Respect
Every 3 Tallow: 2 Respect
[icons: brine_flats, well, tallow]

--- Page 33 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.33
Whenever the Weather Cube shows Fog, the player with the fewest Cart Roads must move their Drover one hex toward the nearest Barley Rise, unless the Tallow Mines is Fallow.
[icons: cart_road, barley_rise, tallow_mines]

--- Page 34 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.5
The preceding rule does not apply if the Loamfields is Fallow. In that case, Mike must instead return one Cart Road to their supply.
[icons: loamfields]

--- Page 35 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.38
The preceding rule does not apply if the Weather Cube has not been flipped since the Production Phase. In that case, Dana must instead discard one Wicker for each Wattle Fence they control.
[icons: wicker, fence]

--- Page 36 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.36
When a player holds more Tallow than Brine, that player must take one Brine from the supply, unless the Brine Flats is Fallow.
[icons: tallow, brine, brine_flats]

--- Page 37 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE N-5
Every 3 Wicker: 3 Respect
Every 3 Brine: −7 Respect
Control of the Brine Flats: 6 Respect
Each Tithe Barn: 4 Respect
[icons: wicker, brine, brine_flats, tithe_barn]

--- Page 38 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE V-10
Every 3 Mutton: 5 Respect
Control of the Peat Bog: 3 Respect
Each Respect point: 5 Respect
Each Favor of the Reeve: 5 Respect
[icons: mutton, peat_bog, respect, favor]

--- Page 39 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
CLARIFICATION
"Adjacent" means sharing an edge, not a corner.

--- Page 40 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.40
Whenever the Weather Cube shows Fog, Mike must draw one Resource card, unless it is the first round of Frostmonth.

--- Page 41 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SEASONAL RULE: SOWING
During this season, the Brine Flats produces Loam instead of its usual resource, and the Tinker moves twice during Almanac Upkeep.
[icons: brine_flats, loam, tinker]

--- Page 42 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.69
The preceding rule does not apply if a Tithe Barn is within two hexes. In that case, Mike must instead pay one Brine to the Tax Assessor.
[icons: brine]

--- Page 43 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.2
The preceding rule does not apply if it is the first round of the game. In that case, Mike must instead move their Drover one hex toward the nearest Brine Flats.
[icons: brine_flats]

--- Page 44 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE E-1
Every 3 Loam: 6 Respect
Each Cottage: 4 Respect
Each Weather Cube: −7 Respect
Control of the Loamfields: 7 Respect
[icons: loam, cottage, weather_cube, loamfields]

--- Page 45 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.45
If a player rolls a 7, the player to the left of the active player must take a Favor of the Reeve, unless a Tithe Barn is within two hexes.
[icons: favor]

--- Page 46 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.26
The preceding rule does not apply if it is the first round of the game. In that case, Dana must instead take a Weather Cube.
[icons: weather_cube]

--- Page 47 · EXAMPLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Rosa has 8 Mutton and a Favor of the Reeve. During the Levy, the Tax Assessor moves onto their Wicker Marsh. Because a Cottage is placed next to the Tallow Mines, Mike must consult the Almanac (see Appendix J).
Everyone agrees this is fair.
[icons: mutton, favor, wicker_marsh, cottage]

--- Page 48 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.68
The preceding rule does not apply if the Weather Cube has not been flipped since the Build Phase. In that case, Rosa must instead roll the Harvest Die again.

--- Page 49 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Does a Hay Cart count as a building?
A: For scoring, yes. For the Levy, no. For the Almanac, it depends.

--- Page 50 · EXAMPLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Mike has 4 Tallow and a Ledger page. During the Production Phase, the Tax Assessor moves onto their Brine Flats. Because a player builds their third Tithe Barn, Dana must skip Almanac Upkeep.
Everyone agrees this is fair.
[icons: tallow, ledger, brine_flats, tithe_barn]

--- Page 51 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Does a Hay Cart count as a building?
A: For scoring, yes. For the Levy, no. For the Almanac, it depends.

--- Page 52 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.52
If the Tax Assessor ends its movement on the Tallow Mines, Mike must discard one Loam for each Hay Cart they control, unless the Weather Cube has not been flipped since Almanac Upkeep.
[icons: tallow_mines, loam, cart]

--- Page 53 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SEASONAL RULE: SOWING
During this season, the Tallow Mines produces Loam instead of its usual resource, and the Tax Assessor moves twice during Dusk Accounting.
[icons: tallow_mines, loam, assessor]

--- Page 54 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.13
The preceding rule does not apply if they have no Resource cards. In that case, Mike must instead skip the Dawn Muster.

--- Page 55 · EXAMPLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Rosa has 2 Tallow and a Weather Cube. During the Dawn Muster, the Tax Assessor moves onto their Brine Flats. Because two players want to build on the same spot, Mike must place a Respect point on the Flax Fields.
Nobody scores this round.
[icons: tallow, weather_cube, brine_flats, respect]

--- Page 56 · EXAMPLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXAMPLE OF PLAY
Mike has 3 Brine and a Ledger page. During the Trade Phase, the Tax Assessor moves onto their Brine Flats. Because a player completes a Cart Road of five or more, Rosa must take a Almanac card.
Mike records it on a Ledger page. Play continues.
[icons: brine, ledger, brine_flats, almanac]

--- Page 57 · COMPONENT · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
COMPONENT SPOTLIGHT: WEATHER CUBE
Each copy of Hearthlands includes 19 Weather Cubes. Spares are available from the publisher.
[icons: weather_cube]

--- Page 58 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.58
If a player builds their third Well, Mike must move their Drover one hex toward the nearest Brine Flats, unless they already built this turn.
[icons: well, brine_flats]

--- Page 59 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.46
The preceding rule does not apply if the Brine Flats is Fallow. In that case, Dana must instead pay one Tallow to the Tax Assessor.
[icons: brine_flats, tallow]

--- Page 60 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE F-5
Control of the Loamfields: −6 Respect
Control of the Tallow Mines: 4 Respect
Every 3 Brine: 4 Respect
Every 3 Wicker: 1 Respect
[icons: loamfields, tallow_mines, brine, wicker]

--- Page 61 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.61
When a player builds their third Tithe Barn, that player must consult the Almanac (see Appendix J), unless the hex is occupied.
[icons: tithe_barn]

--- Page 62 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
HYDRATION REMINDER
Hearthlands is best enjoyed with a beverage.
Beverages are not a resource.
[icons: well]

--- Page 63 · COMPONENT · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
COMPONENT SPOTLIGHT: ALMANAC CARD
Each copy of Hearthlands includes 7 Almanac cards. Spares are available from the publisher.
[icons: almanac]

--- Page 64 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
CLARIFICATION
Resources gained during Almanac Upkeep may be spent in the same turn.

--- Page 65 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.65
If a trade involves exactly one Peat, Dana must discard one Tallow for each Tithe Barn they control, unless they hold a Favor of the Reeve.
[icons: peat, tallow, tithe_barn]

--- Page 66 · NOTE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
We wanted resource trading to feel fair but tense. Playtesting showed four players is the sweet spot.

--- Page 67 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
CLARIFICATION
The Tax Assessor does not collect from Mills.

--- Page 68 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SEASONAL RULE: HIGH HARVEST
During this season, the Tallow Mines produces Brine instead of its usual resource, and the Tinker moves twice during the Build Phase.
[icons: tallow_mines, brine, tinker]

--- Page 69 · FAQ · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Can I trade Tallow for Brine during the Levy?
A: Only if the Reeve is not on either hex involved.
[icons: tallow, brine]

--- Page 70 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE L-6
Control of the Tallow Mines: −4 Respect
Every 3 Wicker: −4 Respect
Every 3 Tallow: 5 Respect
Control of the Mutton Downs: 7 Respect
[icons: tallow_mines, wicker, tallow, mutton_downs]

--- Page 71 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 1.71
If a player builds their third Mill, the active player must discard one Loam for each Drover they control, unless a Tithe Barn is within two hexes.
[icons: mill, loam, drover]

--- Page 72 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
CLARIFICATION
"Adjacent" means sharing an edge, not a corner.

CHAPTER 2 OF 42: Resources and Production

--- Page 73 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 2.1
Whenever a player completes a Cart Road of five or more, the player to the left of the active player must skip Almanac Upkeep, unless they already built this turn.

--- Page 74 · ERRATA · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
EXCEPTION TO RULE 1.61
The preceding rule does not apply if it is the first round of the game. In that case, Dana must instead move the Reeve to another hex.

--- Page 75 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 2.3
If a player holds more Brine than Tallow, the active player must pass one Resource card to the player on their left.
[icons: brine, tallow]

--- Page 76 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE N-4
Each Resource card: 1 Respect
Each Drover: 4 Respect
Each Resource card: 4 Respect
Control of the Peat Bog: 4 Respect
[icons: card_back, drover, peat_bog]

--- Page 77 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
RULE 2.5

[icons: card_back, loamfields]

--- Page 78 · RULE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SEASONAL RULE: HARVEST
During this season, the Flax Fields produces Loam instead of its usual resource, and the Tax Assessor moves twice during the Levy.
[icons: flax_fields, loam, assessor]

--- Page 79 · CLARIFICATION · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
CLARIFICATION
A Granary replaces the Cottage it upgrades. Return the Cottage to your supply.

--- Page 80 · TABLE · Tier 1 (Fussy) ---
SCORING TABLE E-5
Each Granary: 4 Respect
Every 3 Brine: 2 Respect
Every 3 Flax: 5 Respect
Each Well: 6 Respect
[icons: granary, brine, flax, well]

FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTION
Q: Can two Cottages be placed next to each other?

--- Page 226 · NOTE · Tier 2 (Goose-obsessed) ---
DESIGNER'S NOTE
We considered removing this rule, but it holds up four other rules structurally.

[icons: mill]
